摘要改善了，第一步仍未通过

新旧提示词各 9 次：关键要求完整 83→97 / 111；扣除整篇拒收，可交付 73→74 / 111。新提示词还出现混合说话人与时间字段错误，不推广。

三个已暴露开发案例，14 个论证、37 条要求，共 222 次逐项判断；487 条输出全部阅读。标注由同一助手完成，不是独立人工验收。先前诊断与本轮分开保存。

pass 只指本项要求；同一记录仍可能有另一字段错误。error 包括必要上下文缺失与错误元数据，不等同于虚构商业事实。disputed 不计通过。引用范围用于导航，不自动判语义。

coreweave.1.candidate · 10/10 · 交付

旧 GPU 仍有需求，但样本不能代表整支机队

cw_life.1 · pass · Intrator 披露 CoreWeave 的 A100 使用合同延续至 2029；不是 NVIDIA 新芯片出货合同。

保留反方对折旧与寿命的质疑

cw_accounting.1 · pass · Burry 在未注明年份的 November 指控 hyperscalers 在 2026–2028 年少计 1760 亿美元折旧；这是指控，不是审计结论。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p007:s01 Michael Burry accused hyperscalers in November of understating depreciation by $176 billion between 2026 and 2028 by stretching GPU useful-life assumptions to five or six years against a chip cadence that now delivers a new Nvidia architecture roughly every year.

p007o01: Michael Burry accused hyperscalers of understating depreciation by $176 billion over 2026–2028 by assuming GPUs would be useful for five or six years, despite Nvidia now delivering a new architecture roughly every year.

cw_accounting.2 · pass · 未具名 Google 架构师于 2024 年估计 GPU 服务寿命 1–3 年；记者关于融资担忧的背景不是该架构师的话。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p007:s02 Concerns that accelerating GPU obsolescence could unravel AI financing predate his estimate, and an unnamed Google architect put datacenter GPU service life at one to three years back in 2024.

p007o03: An unnamed Google architect put datacenter GPU service life at one to three years in 2024.

基础设施限制使旧卡继续有用

cw_infrastructure.1 · pass · DGX A100 系统最大 6.5kW，能装入约 20kW/机架的旧机房；NVL72 机架需 120–140kW 和液冷；两种功率口径不同。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p009:s01 An air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 system draws 6.5kW at maximum load and fits comfortably in legacy data center halls designed for roughly 20kW per rack.
p009:s02 Nvidia's current GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120kW to 140kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling , roughly six times what those older facilities can feed and cool.

p009o01: An air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 system draws up to 6.5 kW and fits in legacy data-center halls designed for roughly 20 kW per rack.

p009o02: Nvidia's current GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120–140 kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling, roughly six times the power older facilities can supply and cool.

cw_infrastructure.2 · pass · 作者认为 Blackwell 替换旧机房的 Ampere 需要重建电力与冷却；现有通电空间继续出租 A100 胜于闲置。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p009:s03 Blackwell therefore can't move into the halls where Ampere is currently deployed without a rebuild of power delivery and cooling infrastructure.
p010:s01 That mismatch is helping to keep A100 fleets in service regardless of how gracefully or not silicon itself ages.
p010:s02 The energized, air-cooled capacity holding them has no higher-value use, and renting nine-year-old GPUs is a better alternative to leaving it in the dark.

p009o03: Tom's Hardware says Blackwell GPUs cannot be installed in halls where Ampere is deployed without rebuilding power delivery and cooling infrastructure.

p010o01: Tom's Hardware says the mismatch between newer GPUs' infrastructure requirements and older facilities helps keep A100 fleets in service, regardless of how well the silicon itself ages.

p010o02: Tom's Hardware says the energized, air-cooled capacity holding A100 GPUs has no higher-value use, making rentals of nine-year-old GPUs a better alternative to leaving that capacity idle.

p006o02: CoreWeave reported second-quarter revenue of $2.58 billion, up 112% year over year, and a $104 billion revenue backlog that excludes more than $25 billion in new commitments booked since July.

cw_demand.2 · pass · CoreWeave 签约电力 Q2 为 3.7GW，Monday 为 4.2GW；实际上线 1.5GW。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

基础设施限制使旧卡继续有用

cw_infrastructure.1 · pass · DGX A100 系统最大 6.5kW，能装入约 20kW/机架的旧机房；NVL72 机架需 120–140kW 和液冷；两种功率口径不同。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p009:s01 An air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 system draws 6.5kW at maximum load and fits comfortably in legacy data center halls designed for roughly 20kW per rack.
p009:s02 Nvidia's current GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120kW to 140kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling , roughly six times what those older facilities can feed and cool.

p009o01: An air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 system draws 6.5kW at maximum load and fits in legacy data center halls designed for roughly 20kW per rack.

p009o02: Nvidia's current GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120kW to 140kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling, roughly six times what older facilities can feed and cool.

cw_infrastructure.2 · pass · 作者认为 Blackwell 替换旧机房的 Ampere 需要重建电力与冷却；现有通电空间继续出租 A100 胜于闲置。

p009:s03 Blackwell therefore can't move into the halls where Ampere is currently deployed without a rebuild of power delivery and cooling infrastructure.
p010:s01 That mismatch is helping to keep A100 fleets in service regardless of how gracefully or not silicon itself ages.
p010:s02 The energized, air-cooled capacity holding them has no higher-value use, and renting nine-year-old GPUs is a better alternative to leaving it in the dark.

p009o03: The article argues Blackwell cannot move into the halls where Ampere is deployed without rebuilding power delivery and cooling infrastructure.

p010o02: The article argues that the energized, air-cooled capacity holding A100s has no higher-value use and that renting nine-year-old GPUs is better than leaving that capacity unused.

基础设施限制使旧卡继续有用

cw_infrastructure.1 · pass · DGX A100 系统最大 6.5kW，能装入约 20kW/机架的旧机房；NVL72 机架需 120–140kW 和液冷；两种功率口径不同。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p009:s01 An air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 system draws 6.5kW at maximum load and fits comfortably in legacy data center halls designed for roughly 20kW per rack.
p009:s02 Nvidia's current GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120kW to 140kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling , roughly six times what those older facilities can feed and cool.

p009o01: Tom's Hardware says an air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 draws up to 6.5kW and fits legacy halls designed for roughly 20kW per rack, whereas current Nvidia GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120kW to 140kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling; Blackwell therefore cannot be installed in halls where Ampere is deployed without rebuilding power delivery and cooling infrastructure.

cw_infrastructure.2 · pass · 作者认为 Blackwell 替换旧机房的 Ampere 需要重建电力与冷却；现有通电空间继续出租 A100 胜于闲置。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p009:s03 Blackwell therefore can't move into the halls where Ampere is currently deployed without a rebuild of power delivery and cooling infrastructure.
p010:s01 That mismatch is helping to keep A100 fleets in service regardless of how gracefully or not silicon itself ages.
p010:s02 The energized, air-cooled capacity holding them has no higher-value use, and renting nine-year-old GPUs is a better alternative to leaving it in the dark.

p009o01: Tom's Hardware says an air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 draws up to 6.5kW and fits legacy halls designed for roughly 20kW per rack, whereas current Nvidia GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120kW to 140kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling; Blackwell therefore cannot be installed in halls where Ampere is deployed without rebuilding power delivery and cooling infrastructure.

p010o01: Tom's Hardware says the mismatch between newer GPUs’ power and cooling requirements and legacy facilities helps keep A100 fleets in service regardless of silicon aging, because the energized, air-cooled capacity has no higher-value use and renting nine-year-old GPUs is better than leaving it unused.

p009:s01 An air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 system draws 6.5kW at maximum load and fits comfortably in legacy data center halls designed for roughly 20kW per rack.
p009:s02 Nvidia's current GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120kW to 140kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling , roughly six times what those older facilities can feed and cool.

cw_infrastructure.2 · pass · 作者认为 Blackwell 替换旧机房的 Ampere 需要重建电力与冷却；现有通电空间继续出租 A100 胜于闲置。

p009:s03 Blackwell therefore can't move into the halls where Ampere is currently deployed without a rebuild of power delivery and cooling infrastructure.
p010:s01 That mismatch is helping to keep A100 fleets in service regardless of how gracefully or not silicon itself ages.
p010:s02 The energized, air-cooled capacity holding them has no higher-value use, and renting nine-year-old GPUs is a better alternative to leaving it in the dark.

p009o01: The article says an air-cooled Nvidia DGX A100 draws up to 6.5 kW and fits legacy halls designed for roughly 20 kW per rack, while Nvidia GB200 and GB300 NVL72 racks draw 120–140 kW and require direct-to-chip liquid cooling; therefore, Blackwell cannot use halls where Ampere is deployed without rebuilding power delivery and cooling infrastructure.

p010o01: The article argues that power and cooling mismatch keeps A100 fleets in service regardless of how the silicon ages: energized, air-cooled capacity has no higher-value use, so renting nine-year-old GPUs is better than leaving it unused.

mf_market.1 · pass · TSMC 将 2030 年全球半导体市场预期由 1 万亿提高到 1.5 万亿美元，并预计 AI/HPC 占该机会 55%。

p008:s01 Nvidia's foundry partner TSMC recently noted that the global semiconductor market's revenue could reach a whopping $1.5 trillion in 2030.
p008:s02 The Taiwan-based foundry giant had previously anticipated $1 trillion in semiconductor revenue by the end of the decade.
p008:s03 However, AI-fueled demand for chips led to a substantial upgrade to its guidance.
p009:s01 TSMC points out that AI and high-performance computing (HPC) chips will account for 55% of this lucrative opportunity.

p008o01: TSMC said global semiconductor-market revenue could reach $1.5 trillion in 2030, revising its prior end-of-decade expectation of $1 trillion upward because AI-fueled chip demand had substantially increased.

p009o01: TSMC said AI and high-performance-computing chips would represent 55% of the $1.5 trillion semiconductor-market opportunity it projected for 2030.

mf_market.2 · pass · 作者用上述 2030 年规模与份额推得 8250 亿美元 AI 数据中心芯片市场机会；不是 NVIDIA 收入。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p008:s01 Nvidia's foundry partner TSMC recently noted that the global semiconductor market's revenue could reach a whopping $1.5 trillion in 2030.
p009:s01 TSMC points out that AI and high-performance computing (HPC) chips will account for 55% of this lucrative opportunity.
p009:s02 That puts Nvidia's addressable opportunity in the AI data center chip market at an impressive $825 billion.

p008o01: TSMC said global semiconductor-market revenue could reach $1.5 trillion in 2030, revising its prior end-of-decade expectation of $1 trillion upward because AI-fueled chip demand had substantially increased.

竞争者在部分任务上有吸引力

换美国供应商不能消除政治依赖

rw_politics.1 · pass · Winter-Levy 说分散 NVIDIA 供应商不能消除美国政治风险；中国尚不能规模供应竞争芯片，美国准入又要求排除中国硬件。

p007:s01 “Diversification away from Nvidia reduces the Gulf’s dependence on any one commercial vendor, but it doesn’t change the political risk of dependence on the U.S. at all,” Sam Winter-Levy, a fellow at the think tank Carnegie Endowment for International Peace, who studies the geopolitics of chips, told Rest of World .
p007:s02 “What would change that is Chinese supply, but China cannot yet export competitive chips at scale, and the U.S. has made the Gulf’s access to U.S.
p007:s03 AI tech conditional on keeping Chinese hardware out.”

共同供应瓶颈限制资金和换供应商的效果

rw_supply.1 · disputed · Dimmich 说 NVIDIA 与对手共享高速内存和 TSMC 制造产能瓶颈。

共享供应商识别正确，但 downstream 与供应链方向不符，需单列争议。

p019:s01 Buying from a rival does not sidestep Nvidia in any case, because its competitors depend on the same suppliers further down the chain, Dimmich said.
p019:s02 The two hardest things to secure are a specialized high-speed memory that keeps the chips fed with data, and space in the factories run by Taiwanese contract manufacturer TSMC, which makes chips for Nvidia and its rivals alike.

p019o01: Kamil Dimmich said buying from Nvidia rivals does not remove supply-chain dependence because competitors also rely on the same downstream suppliers; the hardest inputs to secure are specialized high-speed memory and capacity at Taiwan’s TSMC factories, which make chips for Nvidia and its rivals.

rw_supply.2 · pass · Dimmich 认为美国科技买家也能融资，海湾财富目前不保证排队优势；资本将来可能成为约束，不是永久无约束。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p023:s01 Even the Gulf’s great wealth buys less of an edge than it might expect, Dimmich told Rest of World , because the deep-pocketed U.S. technology giants it competes with for chips can raise just as much money.
p023:s02 Cash is not what decides who gets to the front of the line, he said.
p024:s01 “I don’t think infinite money is much of an advantage at the moment,” said Dimmich.
p024:s02 “All the U.S. buyers of this equipment seem to be able to access infinite amounts in capital markets at extremely high valuations, so that’s not been a constraint, although it may become so in future.”

p023o01: Kamil Dimmich said Gulf wealth gives it less of an advantage than it might expect against deep-pocketed U.S. technology companies competing for chips, which can raise comparable sums, and that cash does not determine who reaches the front of the line.

p024o01: Kamil Dimmich said unlimited money is not much of an advantage now because U.S. equipment buyers can access very large amounts of capital at extremely high valuations; he qualified that this may become a constraint in the future.

rw_build.1 · pass · Humain 正在 Riyadh、Dammam 建数据中心，计划使用数十万 NVIDIA 芯片；G42 Abu Dhabi Stargate 首期将使用 40 万片。

p008:s03 Humain is also building data centers in Riyadh and Dammam, powered by several hundred thousand Nvidia chips.
p009:s01 The UAE is investing on the same scale through its state-backed AI firm G42, which is building a data center in Abu Dhabi called Stargate.
p009:s02 Its first stage will run on 400,000 Nvidia chips .

p008o03: Humain is building data centers in Riyadh and Dammam that are to be powered by several hundred thousand Nvidia chips.

rw_build.2 · pass · Peng Xiao 在 January 采访称 Stargate 大部分机器仍是 NVIDIA，虽曾尝试分散供应商；January 年份未明确。

p009o02: G42 has spent months trying to spread orders among several suppliers, but CEO Peng Xiao said in an interview that Stargate’s machines would be “mostly” Nvidia.

rw_build.4 · pass · NVIDIA 截至 October 的季度数据中心收入 512 亿美元，同比增 66%，年份未明确；公司称 Blackwell sold out。

p011:s01 The scale of Nvidia’s hold was evident in the quarter ending October, when its data center business brought in a record $51.2 billion , up 66% from the previous year.
p011:s02 Its newest Blackwell computing architecture line is “sold out,” Nvidia said in the earnings report.

竞争者在部分任务上有吸引力

rw_competition.1 · pass · Humain 在 last August 与 AMD 签 100 亿美元/500MW 协议，与 Groq 签 20 亿协议，与 Qualcomm 合作 200MW；不同供应商不同合同。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p014:s02 Last August, it struck a $10 billion deal with AMD for 500 megawatts of computing power, a $2 billion agreement with Groq to run its chatbot, and a tie-up with Qualcomm to fill a Saudi data center with 200 megawatts of its chips.

p014o01: Humain has tried to widen its supplier list; last August, it struck a $10 billion deal with AMD for 500 megawatts of computing power, a $2 billion agreement with Groq to run its chatbot, and a Qualcomm tie-up to supply 200 megawatts of chips to a Saudi data center.

rw_competition.2 · pass · Dimmich 认为 Qualcomm/Groq 能便宜运行完成训练的模型，但训练强模型仍需 NVIDIA；ASIC 推理可更便宜，仍依赖 TSMC。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p015:s02 The Qualcomm and Groq chips are built to run finished AI models cheaply, for everyday tasks like answering a user’s questions, while training a powerful model still calls for Nvidia’s chips, Kamil Dimmich, a partner at emerging-markets investment firm North of South Capital, told Rest of World .
p020:s01 “Nvidia chips are essential for training,” Dimmich said.
p020:s02 “For this type of inference compute, increasingly there are ASIC [application-specific integrated circuit] designs that are cheaper than Nvidia but still need foundry capacity at TSMC.”

p015o02: Kamil Dimmich said Qualcomm and Groq chips are designed to run finished AI models cheaply for everyday tasks such as answering questions, while training a powerful model still calls for Nvidia chips.

p020o01: Kamil Dimmich said Nvidia chips are essential for training, while increasingly available ASIC designs for this type of inference compute are cheaper than Nvidia chips but still require TSMC foundry capacity.

软件迁移成本支撑竞争优势

rw_cuda.1 · pass · NVIDIA 用近二十年构建 CUDA 工具，开发者超过 400 万；换到竞争平台意味着重建多年工作。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p012:s01 Part of that hold is the chip itself, and part is the Compute Unified Device Architecture , Nvidia’s proprietary parallel computing platform.
p012:s02 Nvidia spent almost two decades building the tools engineers use to run AI on its chips, and more than 4 million developers now work in the CUDA system.
p012:s03 Moving to a rival would mean rebuilding years of work from scratch.

p012o01: The article says Nvidia’s hold on AI chips reflects both its chip and its proprietary CUDA parallel-computing platform: Nvidia spent almost two decades building tools for running AI on its chips, more than 4 million developers now work in CUDA, and moving to a rival would mean rebuilding years of work from scratch.

换美国供应商不能消除政治依赖

rw_politics.1 · pass · Winter-Levy 说分散 NVIDIA 供应商不能消除美国政治风险；中国尚不能规模供应竞争芯片，美国准入又要求排除中国硬件。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p007:s01 “Diversification away from Nvidia reduces the Gulf’s dependence on any one commercial vendor, but it doesn’t change the political risk of dependence on the U.S. at all,” Sam Winter-Levy, a fellow at the think tank Carnegie Endowment for International Peace, who studies the geopolitics of chips, told Rest of World .
p007:s02 “What would change that is Chinese supply, but China cannot yet export competitive chips at scale, and the U.S. has made the Gulf’s access to U.S.
p007:s03 AI tech conditional on keeping Chinese hardware out.”

p007o01: Sam Winter-Levy said diversification from Nvidia reduces Gulf dependence on a single commercial vendor but does not reduce political dependence on the United States; Chinese supply could change that, but China cannot yet export competitive chips at scale and U.S. access to AI technology is conditional on excluding Chinese hardware.

rw_politics.2 · pass · AMD、Qualcomm 同为美国产品，需要 Washington 批准后才能向海湾销售。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p017:s01 Chips from AMD or Qualcomm come with the same strings attached as those from Nvidia, because all of them are U.S. products that need Washington’s approval before they can be sold to the Gulf, Winter-Levy said.

p017o01: Sam Winter-Levy said AMD and Qualcomm chips carry the same U.S. approval requirement as Nvidia chips because they are U.S. products that need Washington’s approval before sale to the Gulf.

rw_politics.3 · pass · Winter-Levy 也说海湾资金、持股及建设速度带来真实议价权；Soliman 认为其选择是深化美国合作，不是追求昂贵自主。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p027:s01 Still, the Gulf’s money, stakes in U.S. technology companies, and speed in building data centers give it real bargaining power, Winter-Levy said.
p027:s03 Its leaders, Soliman said, know exactly what they are choosing.
p028:s01 “The Gulf’s current path is the opposite of diversification,” he said.
p028:s02 “They are accelerating integration through massive data centers and direct U.S. partnerships rather than chasing expensive and uncertain independence.
p028:s03 The Gulf states are AI realists.”

p027o01: Sam Winter-Levy said the Gulf’s money, stakes in U.S. technology companies, and speed in building data centers give it real bargaining power.

p027o03: Mohammed Soliman said Gulf leaders know exactly what they are choosing.

p028o01: Mohammed Soliman said the Gulf’s current path is the opposite of diversification: Gulf states are accelerating integration through massive data centers and direct U.S. partnerships rather than pursuing expensive, uncertain independence, and are “AI realists.”

共同供应瓶颈限制资金和换供应商的效果

rw_supply.1 · disputed · Dimmich 说 NVIDIA 与对手共享高速内存和 TSMC 制造产能瓶颈。

共享供应商识别正确，但 downstream 与供应链方向不符，需单列争议。

p019:s01 Buying from a rival does not sidestep Nvidia in any case, because its competitors depend on the same suppliers further down the chain, Dimmich said.
p019:s02 The two hardest things to secure are a specialized high-speed memory that keeps the chips fed with data, and space in the factories run by Taiwanese contract manufacturer TSMC, which makes chips for Nvidia and its rivals alike.

p019o01: Kamil Dimmich said buying from a rival does not sidestep Nvidia because its competitors rely on the same downstream suppliers; he identified specialized high-speed memory and capacity at Taiwanese contract manufacturer TSMC, which makes chips for Nvidia and its rivals, as the two hardest things to secure.

rw_supply.2 · pass · Dimmich 认为美国科技买家也能融资，海湾财富目前不保证排队优势；资本将来可能成为约束，不是永久无约束。

助手读完整篇及本次输出后确认：该项所需含义保留；允许多条记录，不以条数加分。

p023:s01 Even the Gulf’s great wealth buys less of an edge than it might expect, Dimmich told Rest of World , because the deep-pocketed U.S. technology giants it competes with for chips can raise just as much money.
p023:s02 Cash is not what decides who gets to the front of the line, he said.
p024:s01 “I don’t think infinite money is much of an advantage at the moment,” said Dimmich.
p024:s02 “All the U.S. buyers of this equipment seem to be able to access infinite amounts in capital markets at extremely high valuations, so that’s not been a constraint, although it may become so in future.”

p023o01: Kamil Dimmich said Gulf wealth buys less of an edge than the Gulf might expect because deep-pocketed U.S. technology giants competing for chips can raise just as much money; he said cash does not determine who gets to the front of the line.

p024o01: Kamil Dimmich said unlimited money is not much of an advantage at present because U.S. buyers of the equipment appear able to access unlimited capital at extremely high valuations; he said this has not been a constraint, although it may become one in future.

共同供应瓶颈限制资金和换供应商的效果

rw_supply.1 · disputed · Dimmich 说 NVIDIA 与对手共享高速内存和 TSMC 制造产能瓶颈。

downstream 的供应链方向存疑，不能算无争议通过。

p019:s01 Buying from a rival does not sidestep Nvidia in any case, because its competitors depend on the same suppliers further down the chain, Dimmich said.
p019:s02 The two hardest things to secure are a specialized high-speed memory that keeps the chips fed with data, and space in the factories run by Taiwanese contract manufacturer TSMC, which makes chips for Nvidia and its rivals alike.

p019o01: Kamil Dimmich said buying from a rival does not sidestep Nvidia because competitors depend on the same downstream suppliers; the two hardest things to secure are specialized high-speed memory that keeps chips fed with data and space in factories run by Taiwanese contract manufacturer TSMC, which makes chips for Nvidia and its rivals.

rw_supply.2 · pass · Dimmich 认为美国科技买家也能融资，海湾财富目前不保证排队优势；资本将来可能成为约束，不是永久无约束。

p023:s01 Even the Gulf’s great wealth buys less of an edge than it might expect, Dimmich told Rest of World , because the deep-pocketed U.S. technology giants it competes with for chips can raise just as much money.
p023:s02 Cash is not what decides who gets to the front of the line, he said.
p024:s01 “I don’t think infinite money is much of an advantage at the moment,” said Dimmich.
p024:s02 “All the U.S. buyers of this equipment seem to be able to access infinite amounts in capital markets at extremely high valuations, so that’s not been a constraint, although it may become so in future.”

p023o01: Kamil Dimmich said the Gulf’s great wealth buys less of an edge than it might expect because deep-pocketed U.S. technology giants competing for chips can raise just as much money, and cash does not decide who gets to the front of the line.

p024o01: Kamil Dimmich said infinite money is not much of an advantage at the moment: U.S. buyers appear able to access infinite capital-market funding at extremely high valuations, so capital has not been a constraint, although it may become one in future.
